5224	analysisWe need to produce a concise summary of the method transferMetadata. The code: public void transferMetadata (DatasetNode from,boolean parentsAlso){ if (parentsAlso){ ThreddsMetadata inherit = getInheritableMetadata ();// make sure exists inheritMetadata (from,inherit.getFlds ());} // local metadata for (Map.Entry < String,Object > entry:from.getFldIterator ()){ if (parentsAlso && entry.getKey ().equals (Dataset.ThreddsMetadataInheritable))continue;// already did this if (Dataset.listFlds.contains (entry.getKey ()))addToNewList (flds,entry.getKey (),entry.getValue ());else flds.put (entry.getKey (),entry.getValue ());} // tmi must be mutable, transfer if not ThreddsMetadata tmiOld = (ThreddsMetadata)get (Dataset.ThreddsMetadataInheritable);if (tmiOld != null && tmiOld.isImmutable ()){ ThreddsMetadata tmiNew = new ThreddsMetadata (tmiOld);flds.put (Dataset.ThreddsMetadataInheritable,tmiNew);} }

Essentially: transfer metadata from a DatasetNode to current object's fields, optionally including inheritable parents metadata. It copies fields, handles list fields, ensures mutable metadata.

We need 15 tokens approx. Something like: "Copy dataset node metadata, optionally including inherited fields, ensuring mutable metadata." Count tokens: Copy(1) dataset(2) node(3) metadata,(4) optionally(5) including(6) inherited(7) fields,(8) ensuring(9) mutable(10) metadata(11). That's 11 tokens. Good.

But maybe mention "transferMetadata" summarization: "Transfer metadata from node, optionally inheriting parents, ensuring mutable copy." Count: Transfer(1) metadata(2) from(3) node,(4) optionally(5) inheriting(6) parents,(7) ensuring(8) mutable(9) copy(10). 10 tokens.

We need to naturalize identifiers: dataset node, parentsAlso, ThreddsMetadata, etc. So "Transfer metadata from a dataset node, optionally inheriting parent metadata, ensuring mutable copy." Count: Transfer(1) metadata(2) from(3) a(4) dataset(5) node,(6) optionally(7) inheriting(8) parent(9) metadata,(10) ensuring(11) mutable(12)
